# Java Audiveris 5.11 (Temurin JDK 25) HEADS prolog oracle.
#
# Every page reaches the real LEDGERS step in a fresh JVM. The probe then
# calls DistancesBuilder.buildDistances() and HeadSpotsBuilder.getSpots()
# in HeadsStep.doProlog source order, stopping before NoteHeadsBuilder.
#
# Staff/ledger/seed input digests cover exact paint-order records, glyph
# bounds and complete run tables. Staff records additionally cover every
# synthetic line-paint x/y range used by DistancesBuilder.paintLines.
# Pixel digests are FNV-1a-64 in row-major byte order. Distance digests
# are FNV-1a-64 over row-major signed i32 values in little-endian order.
# Record digests include a trailing newline. Doubles use toHexString.
# Components retain GlyphFactory's unsorted order. Dispatch records name
# those ordinals exactly; one component may belong to several systems.
#
# Compile and run from app/ with the frozen JDK and saved runtime classpath.
# Generate one target per fresh JVM, in this order: chula, allegretto,
# batuque, carmen, cucaracha, hove, zizi, BachInvention5.
headsprologpage chula.png#1 width 2450 height 1954 systems 3
